0986 — band one arm overhead biceps curl

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0986 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A standing one-arm curl with the arm held overhead against band tension. It trains the biceps.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart and place one end of the band under your foot. Hold the other end of the band with your arm fully extended overhead, palm facing forward.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0986.json#/instructions/0, 0986.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Hold the other end of the band with your arm fully extended overhead, palm facing forward.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0986.json#/instructions/1

Legs Feet

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart and place one end of the band under your foot.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0986.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Resistance band base family. For this standing proposal, the support foot or feet contact a level ground plane; that ground plane is an authored scene choice when the source does not name it. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 0986.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart and place one end of the band under your foot. 0986.json#/instructions/0
  2. Hold the other end of the band with your arm fully extended overhead, palm facing forward. 0986.json#/instructions/1
  3. Keeping your upper arm stationary, curl your forearm towards your shoulder, squeezing your biceps. 0986.json#/instructions/2
  4. Pause for a moment at the top, then slowly lower your forearm back to the starting position. 0986.json#/instructions/3
  5. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions, then switch arms. 0986.json#/instructions/4

0987 — band one arm single leg split squat

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0987 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A single-leg split squat with a band, back foot raised. It trains the quads and glutes.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand with your feet hip-width apart and place a resistance band around your ankles.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0987.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Use a closed hand contact around the source-defined load or control with wrists neutral. Preserve any grip orientation, hand count, handle position and arm path stated in the phases; unspecified grip/handle geometry remains for review.

authored_functional_contact; source verified: false. The declared equipment requires a control/load contact, but its precise grip geometry is not supplied here. 0987.json#/equipment

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 0987.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand with your feet hip-width apart and place a resistance band around your ankles. 0987.json#/instructions/0
  2. Extend one leg forward and rest the top of your foot on a bench or step behind you. 0987.json#/instructions/1
  3. Hold onto a support with one hand for balance. 0987.json#/instructions/2
  4. Bend your standing leg and lower your body down into a squat position, keeping your knee in line with your toes. 0987.json#/instructions/3
  5. Push through your heel to return to the starting position. 0987.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions, then switch legs. 0987.json#/instructions/5

0988 — band one arm standing low row

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0988 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A standing one-arm low row with a band. It trains the back and biceps.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Attach the band to a stable anchor point at waist height. Stand facing the anchor point with your feet shoulder-width apart. Hold the band with one hand, palm facing inward, and step back to create tension in the band.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0988.json#/instructions/0, 0988.json#/instructions/1, 0988.json#/instructions/2

Arms Hands

Hold the band with one hand, palm facing inward, and step back to create tension in the band.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0988.json#/instructions/2

Legs Feet

Stand facing the anchor point with your feet shoulder-width apart.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0988.json#/instructions/1

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Resistance band base family, Band anchor point. For this standing proposal, the support foot or feet contact a level ground plane; that ground plane is an authored scene choice when the source does not name it. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 0988.json#/instructions/1

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Attach the band to a stable anchor point at waist height. 0988.json#/instructions/0
  2. Stand facing the anchor point with your feet shoulder-width apart. 0988.json#/instructions/1
  3. Hold the band with one hand, palm facing inward, and step back to create tension in the band. 0988.json#/instructions/2
  4. Bend your knees slightly and hinge forward at the hips, keeping your back straight. 0988.json#/instructions/3
  5. Pull the band towards your waist, squeezing your shoulder blades together. 0988.json#/instructions/4
  6. Pause for a moment at the top of the movement, then slowly release the band back to the starting position. 0988.json#/instructions/5
  7. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions, then switch sides. 0988.json#/instructions/6

0989 — band one arm twisting chest press

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0989 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A one-arm chest press with a band that adds a twist. It trains the chest and core.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Attach the band to a sturdy anchor point at chest height. Stand with your side facing the anchor point and grab the band with one hand.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0989.json#/instructions/0, 0989.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Stand with your side facing the anchor point and grab the band with one hand.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0989.json#/instructions/1

Legs Feet

Use a comfortable hip-width stance with soft knees unless the source specifies a single-leg, split or wider stance; keep the documented support foot grounded.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial leg placement was not explicit; this is a reversible balance/support choice, not a replacement for source movement. 0989.json#/instructions/1

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Resistance band base family, Band anchor point. For this standing proposal, the support foot or feet contact a level ground plane; that ground plane is an authored scene choice when the source does not name it. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 0989.json#/instructions/1

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Attach the band to a sturdy anchor point at chest height. 0989.json#/instructions/0
  2. Stand with your side facing the anchor point and grab the band with one hand. 0989.json#/instructions/1
  3. Step away from the anchor point to create tension in the band. 0989.json#/instructions/2
  4. Position your feet shoulder-width apart and slightly bend your knees. 0989.json#/instructions/3
  5. Bring your hand holding the band across your body, towards the opposite shoulder. 0989.json#/instructions/4
  6. While maintaining tension in the band, push your hand forward and away from your body, extending your arm. 0989.json#/instructions/5
  7. Slowly return to the starting position and repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 0989.json#/instructions/6
  8. Switch sides and repeat the exercise with the other hand. 0989.json#/instructions/7

0990 — band one arm twisting seated row

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0990 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

A seated one-arm row with a band and a torso twist. It trains the back and obliques.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Sit on a bench or chair with your feet flat on the ground and your back straight. Hold the band with one hand and extend your arm fully in front of you.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0990.json#/instructions/0, 0990.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Hold the band with one hand and extend your arm fully in front of you.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0990.json#/instructions/1

Legs Feet

Sit on a bench or chair with your feet flat on the ground and your back straight.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0990.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Resistance band base family, Adjustable utility bench, Household chair. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 0990.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Sit on a bench or chair with your feet flat on the ground and your back straight. 0990.json#/instructions/0
  2. Hold the band with one hand and extend your arm fully in front of you. 0990.json#/instructions/1
  3. Keeping your back straight, pull the band towards your body by bending your elbow and squeezing your shoulder blades together. 0990.json#/instructions/2
  4. At the same time, twist your torso towards the side of the pulling arm. 0990.json#/instructions/3
  5. Pause for a moment at the top, then slowly release the tension in the band and return to the starting position. 0990.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions, then switch to the other arm. 0990.json#/instructions/5

0991 — band pull through

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0991 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A hip hinge pulling a band through the legs. It trains the glutes and hamstrings.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Attach a resistance band to a sturdy anchor point at ground level. Stand facing away from the anchor point with your feet shoulder-width apart.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0991.json#/instructions/0, 0991.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Let the upper arms rest near the torso, elbows soft and hands relaxed; allow small balance movements that do not add a new exercise action.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial arm position was not explicit; this is a neutral staging choice compatible with the inferred support pose. 0991.json#/instructions/1

Legs Feet

Stand facing away from the anchor point with your feet shoulder-width apart.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0991.json#/instructions/1

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Resistance band base family, Band anchor point. For this standing proposal, the support foot or feet contact a level ground plane; that ground plane is an authored scene choice when the source does not name it. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 0991.json#/instructions/1

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Attach a resistance band to a sturdy anchor point at ground level. 0991.json#/instructions/0
  2. Stand facing away from the anchor point with your feet shoulder-width apart. 0991.json#/instructions/1
  3. Step forward to create tension in the band, keeping your knees slightly bent. 0991.json#/instructions/2
  4. Hinge at the hips and push your glutes back, maintaining a slight bend in your knees. 0991.json#/instructions/3
  5. Lower your torso until it is parallel to the ground, feeling a stretch in your hamstrings. 0991.json#/instructions/4
  6. Drive your hips forward and squeeze your glutes to return to the starting position. 0991.json#/instructions/5
  7. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 0991.json#/instructions/6

0992 — band push sit-up

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0992 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: supine.

A sit-up that pushes a band forward as you come up. It trains the abs.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Attach the band securely to a stable anchor point. Lie flat on your back with your knees bent and feet flat on the ground. Hold the band with both hands and extend your arms straight up towards the ceiling.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0992.json#/instructions/0, 0992.json#/instructions/1, 0992.json#/instructions/2

Arms Hands

Hold the band with both hands and extend your arms straight up towards the ceiling.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0992.json#/instructions/2

Legs Feet

Lie flat on your back with your knees bent and feet flat on the ground.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0992.json#/instructions/1

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Resistance band base family, Band anchor point. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 0992.json#/instructions/1

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Attach the band securely to a stable anchor point. 0992.json#/instructions/0
  2. Lie flat on your back with your knees bent and feet flat on the ground. 0992.json#/instructions/1
  3. Hold the band with both hands and extend your arms straight up towards the ceiling. 0992.json#/instructions/2
  4. Engaging your abs, slowly lift your upper body off the ground, curling forward until your torso is at a 45-degree angle. 0992.json#/instructions/3
  5. Pause for a moment at the top, then slowly lower your upper body back down to the starting position. 0992.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 0992.json#/instructions/5

0993 — band reverse fly

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0993 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A reverse fly with a band, opening the arms to the sides. It trains the rear delts and upper back.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Attach the band to a stationary object at chest height. Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart and hold the band with both hands in front of you.

  2. Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart and hold the band with both hands in front of you. 0993.json#/instructions/1
  3. Keep your arms straight and lift them out to the sides until they are parallel to the ground. 0993.json#/instructions/2
  4. Squeeze your shoulder blades together at the top of the movement. 0993.json#/instructions/3
  5. Slowly lower your arms back to the starting position. 0993.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 0993.json#/instructions/5

A seated hip rotation against a band around the thighs. It works the glutes and hip muscles.

1015 — band vertical pallof press

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1015 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A vertical Pallof press with a band, pressing at chest height and holding. It trains the core to resist rotation.

Proposed staging

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Sit in the sled with feet on the platform and release the safety. 10198.json#/instructions/0
  2. Press the platform up with both legs and lock out gently. 10198.json#/instructions/1
  3. Shift the load to one leg and lower it slowly for 3-4 seconds. 10198.json#/instructions/2
  4. Press back up with both legs. 10198.json#/instructions/3
  5. Alternate the lowering leg each rep and never let your lower back round. 10198.json#/instructions/4
